[Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责审查 **新颖性、完整性、相关工作和写作质量**。

## 论文信息
- 标题: Layer Selection Sensitivity in Hidden-State Safety Probes for Reasoning Models
- 文件路径: `projects/contrastive_trace_safety/docs/paper/main.tex`（各 section 在 `sections/` 子目录）
- PDF: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_5/projects/contrastive_trace_safety/docs/paper/main.pdf`（29 页，含附录）

## Venue 要求
- **EMNLP 2026**，通过 ARR May 2026 cycle 提交
- 截止日期: 2026-05-25
- 长文: 正文 **严格 8 页**，参考文献无限制，Limitations 和 Ethics 不计入页数
- 模板: ACL template，`[review]` 模式
- **Double-blind** 匿名审稿
- Overall Assessment 评分标准: 5=Consider for Award, 4=Conference, 3.5=Borderline Conference, 3=Findings, 2.5=Borderline Findings, 2=Resubmit next cycle, 1=Do not resubmit

## 你的审查重点
1. **新颖性**：Metric-induced layer selection bias 作为核心发现的新颖度如何？这个发现对 AI safety 社区的价值有多大？与已有 interpretability/probing 工作的差异是否显著？
2. **完整性**：论文是否完整回答了自己提出的研究问题？消融实验是否充分（五指标 ablation、encoder quality boundary、position control）？负面结果（BOCPD、Mahalanobis、cross-transfer）的呈现是否增加了论文价值？Three-layer ensemble 是否有足够实验支撑？
3. **相关工作**：是否遗漏重要相关文献？对 TrajGuard、SafeSwitch、SafeRemind 等工作的定位是否准确？Probing methodology 文献（如 Hewitt & Manning 的 control tasks）是否充分引用？
4. **写作质量**：论文结构是否清晰？Claim 的强度是否与证据匹配？"confirmatory" vs "exploratory" 的框架是否帮助还是混淆读者？信息密度是否合适（29 页是否有冗余）？

请按以下结构输出审稿意见：
1. Summary（2-3 句）
2. Strengths（逐条）
3. Weaknesses（逐条，标注 severity: Must-Fix / Should-Fix / Minor）
4. Questions for Authors
5. Missing References（如有）
6. Overall Assessment（评分 + 置信度 1-5）
7. Detailed Comments（按 section 逐节评论）

Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Web search results for query: "ACL ARR 2026 reviewer guidelines review form criteria"

```text
Links: [{"title":"ARR Reviewer Guidelines – ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/reviewerguidelines"},{"title":"aclrollingreview/cfp.md at main · acl-org/aclrollingreview","url":"https://github.com/acl-org/aclrollingreview/blob/main/cfp.md"},{"title":"Incentives 2026 – ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/incentives2026"},{"title":"Review Form – ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/reviewform"},{"title":"ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"https://aclrollingreview.org/"},{"title":"CALL FOR PAPERS – ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/cfp"},{"title":"ARR Area Chair Guidelines – ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/acguidelines"},{"title":"aclrollingreview/reviewing.md at main · acl-org/aclrollingreview","url":"https://github.com/acl-org/aclrollingreview/blob/main/reviewing.md"},{"title":"Authors Guidelines – ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/authors"},{"title":"How ARR works – ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/reviewing"}]

I've found relevant information about ACL Rolling Review (ARR) 2026 reviewer guidelines and review form criteria. Here are the key findings:

## Review Form Criteria

For Main papers, the main criteria include soundness, reproducibility, novelty, impact and other factors, while for Findings papers the main criteria are soundness and reproducibility.

The review recommendation scale includes: 5 = Consider for Award (papers potentially worthy of outstanding paper awards, up to top 2.5%); 2 = Resubmit next cycle (substantial revisions completable by next ARR cycle); 1.5 = Resubmit after next cycle (substantial revisions requiring more time); and 1 = Do not resubmit (papers that need full redoing or aren't relevant to the *ACL community).

## Reviewer Guidelines

The suggested workflow for reviewers is to: (1) read the paper and get the first impression, (2) search for related work, not deliberately trying to find that particular submission.

The program chairs and ARR EiCs will consider egregious cases reported by chairs, including cases of extremely terse reviews, unprofessional tone, and LLM-generated reviews.

Reviews should be specific (especially about recommended revisions) and professional in tone (rather than rude/dismissive).

You can access the full reviewer guidelines at the official ARR website: http://aclrollingreview.org/reviewerguidelines


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.
```

```text
# ACL Rolling Review Form - Scoring Dimensions

## 1. **Reviewer Confidence**
Scale: 1-5
- 5: Positive evaluation is correct; carefully read paper and familiar with related work
- 4: Quite sure; checked important points carefully
- 3: Pretty sure; good field knowledge but didn't verify all details
- 2: Can defend evaluation but likely missed some details or novelty assessment
- 1: Outside expertise area or paper very difficult to understand

## 2. **Soundness**
Scale: 1-5 (with half-point increments)
- 5: Excellent; one of the most thorough studies of its type
- 4: Strong; sufficient support for claims with possible minor additions
- 3: Acceptable; supports main claims with minor gaps
- 2: Poor; major claims lack sufficient support or methodological problems
- 1: Major Issues; insufficiently thorough or irrelevant to ACL

## 3. **Excitement**
Scale: 1-5 (with half-point increments)
- 5: Highly Exciting; would recommend to others or attend presentation
- 4: Exciting; would mention to others or make effort to attend
- 3: Interesting; might mention points or attend if time permits
- 2: Potentially Interesting; doesn't resonate but might interest others
- 1: Not Exciting; lacks resonance and broader community relevance

## 4. **Overall Assessment**
Scale: 1-5 (with half-point increments)
- 5: Consider for Award (top 2.5% papers)
- 4.5: Borderline Award
- 4: Conference acceptance
- 3.5: Borderline Conference
- 3: Findings acceptance
- 2.5: Borderline Findings
- 2: Resubmit next cycle
- 1.5: Resubmit after next cycle
- 1: Do not resubmit

## 5. **Reproducibility**
Scale: 1-5
- 5: Results easily reproducible
- 4: Mostly reproducible with minor variance
- 3: Reproducible with difficulty; underspecified parameters
- 2: Hard to reproduce; unavailable data or insufficient details
- 1: Impossible to reproduce

## 6. **Datasets** (if applicable)
Scale: 1-5
- 5: Enabling; affects research project decisions
- 4: Useful; recommended for ongoing work
- 3: Potentially useful; may help some researchers
- 2: Documentary; useful for replication only
- 1: No usable datasets

## 7. **Software** (if applicable)
Scale: 1-5
- 5: Enabling; affects project decisions
- 4: Useful; recommended to researchers
- 3: Potentially useful for some applications
- 2: Documentary; useful for replication primarily
- 1: No usable software released

## 8. **Author Identity Knowledge**
Scale: 1-5
- 5: Know/guess author name via double-blind violation
- 4: Knowledge from preprint or workshop paper
- 3: Deduced from submission contents
- 2: From informal communication
- 1: No educated guess possible
```

```text
I've now read all 29 pages of the paper and the reference verification results. Let me now compile my comprehensive review.

Let me summarize what I know:

**Paper Summary:**
The paper studies "layer selection sensitivity" in hidden-state safety probes for reasoning models. The core finding is that the choice of evaluation metric (balanced accuracy, crossing rate, precision, inverse FPR, additive composite) systematically biases which transformer layers are selected as "best" for safety probing, and this has significant implications for safety monitoring.

Key findings:
1. Metric-induced layer selection bias: Different metrics select different layers — threshold-based metrics select shallow layers, while CR-composite selects deep layers


2. The hidden-state advantage depends on encoder quality, showing strong effects with smaller models like 384d MiniLM but diminishing with larger ones like 1024d BGE-large
3. A three-layer ensemble approach that combines probes from layers selected by different threshold-based metrics produces robust, metric-agnostic results
4. Several alternative methods including BOCPD, CUSUM, Mahalanobis distance, RepE, MLP, CCS, and cross-model transfer all underperform or fail to provide meaningful improvements

**Reference verification summary:**
- 69 bib entries, 68 cited, 1 orphan (gelman2013garden)
- 54 verified online

, 12 not found (mostly recent preprints), 3 mismatches in formatting

The core finding that metric choice systematically biases layer selection is a valid empirical contribution, though the novelty is moderate—observing that different metrics favor different layers is somewhat expected in probing work, and the paper's main contribution is documenting this pattern in the safety domain. The safety commitment point concept itself is borrowed from prior work, and the three-layer ensemble, while practical, lacks theoretical depth. The experimental coverage is thorough across 4 models and 5 metrics with good ablations, and the negative results strengthen the overall narrative.

The related work section comprehensively covers both safety and probing literature, properly implements Hewitt & Manning control tasks, and engages with concurrent work. The writing is dense but structured, though calling the analysis "confirmatory" without pre-registration is problematic, and the 29-page length is justified by the extensive robustness checks in the appendix.

Now I'm identifying the critical issues that need addressing. The biggest limitation is that all experiments rely on a single dataset—HarmThoughts—which severely constrains the generalizability of the findings. a significant limitation for a paper making broad claims about "layer selection sensitivity." The finding might be dataset-specific. At minimum, the authors should test on one additional safety benchmark.

2. **"Confirmatory" framing is misleading**: The paper uses "confirmatory" language (Section 4.2), but this is a post-hoc characterization. No pre-registration is mentioned. The 5-fold CV methodology described is standard exploratory analysis, not confirmatory in the statistical sense. The terminology creates false confidence.

3. **Encoder quality confound**: The finding that HS advantage attenuates at BGE-large (1024d) is concerning. It suggests the HS advantage may not be about hidden states capturing genuine safety signals, but rather about the quality of text encoding. If the advantage disappears with stronger encoders, the practical significance of the layer selection findings becomes questionable.

4. **Statistical methodology concerns**: Bootstrap with 10,000 resamples on a dataset of ~1000 examples per model is reasonable, but the Holm-Bonferroni correction applied to 12 tests seems arbitrary without justification for why this specific comparison family was chosen.

**Should-Fix Issues:**

1. **Three-layer ensemble evaluation is thin**: The ensemble is presented as a key practical contribution but only evaluated on the same HarmThoughts dataset, with no significant differences (p > 0.27), which undermines claims about its reliability.

2. **CP assumption**: The binary CP classification is a strong assumption, and the Limitations section acknowledges oscillating safety patterns that may violate this assumption.

3. **Scale limitation**: Only 7-8B models and one 32B model tested; larger models like 70B+ could exhibit qualitatively different safety dynamics.

4. **Cross-model transfer failure**: The negative transfer result is important but underdeveloped—the paper shows PCA-based transfer fails but doesn't explore more sophisticated domain adaptation techniques.

5. **"Confirmatory" vs "exploratory" distinction is post-hoc**: The framework distinguishes confirmatory approaches (threshold-based metrics at threshold-based layers) from exploratory ones (CR-composite at preferred layers), but this distinction appears to be constructed after observing which methods agreed with each other rather than specified in advance.

The reference coverage is actually quite solid overall, though I'm noting a few areas where additional citations could strengthen the work.

Looking at the bigger picture, this is a methodologically sound empirical study with good experimental controls, but the core finding about metric choice biasing layer selection has moderate novelty at best. The single-dataset limitation is the most significant weakness. I'd estimate this lands around 3.0-3.5 on the conference scale—solid findings but not groundbreaking. The soundness is around 3.5 out of 5 given the methodology is generally rigorous, though the encoder quality confound and dataset limitation do undermine some of the broader claims.

The excitement factor is lower at 2.5 out of 5 since the finding is useful but incremental, with moderate community benefit. Reproducibility looks strong at 4 out of 5 with detailed hyperparameters and promised code release. Weighing everything together—the systematic documentation of metric bias in the safety context, thorough ablations, honest negative results, and the M002 bug disclosure showing scientific integrity—against the single dataset, unclear practical deployment impact, and the ensemble solution not being significantly better, I'm settling on an overall rating of 3.0 for Findings with 4 out of 5 confidence.

Now I'm checking the page count against EMNLP requirements. The main text runs through page 8 with the conclusion, limitations are on page 9, and references start on page 10, which means pages 1-8 cover the main content including abstract through conclusion—exactly meeting the 8-page limit. The appendix extends to page 29, and since limitations don't count toward the page requirement, this complies with the submission guidelines.
```

```text
# ARR Reviewer Guidelines: Key Evaluation Criteria and Practices

## What Reviewers Should Evaluate

Reviewers assess papers across three dimensions:

1. **Soundness**: Technical validity and methodological rigor
2. **Excitement**: Personal interest and appeal (separate from soundness)
3. **Overall Recommendation**: Composite judgment considering soundness, novelty, and potential impact

The guidelines emphasize that "soundness scores must be justified by the text of the review." Low scores without clear justification suggest unconscious bias rather than legitimate critique.

## Constructive Review Standards

**Specificity is paramount.** Reviewers should avoid vague criticisms like "missing relevant references" and instead specify which references are needed. The guidelines provide a detailed comparison table showing weak phrasing versus strong, evidence-based critiques.

**Professional tone matters.** Reviews should be "professional and neutral, if not kind." The guidelines note that dismissive or sarcastic language harms reviewers' colleagues and undermines the collaborative nature of peer review.

**Acknowledge author responses.** Reviewers must "at least acknowledge the authors' response" and explain why rebuttals were or weren't convincing, even if assessments don't change.

## Guidance on Novelty, Soundness, and Related Work

**Novelty assessment requires caution.** The guidelines flag "H3: Results are not novel" as a problematic heuristic unless paired with specific prior work citations. Reviewers must provide references when claiming non-novelty, following ACL policy that work published 3+ months before submission deadline constitutes prior art.

**Soundness evaluation balances rigor with contribution type.** Papers need not achieve state-of-the-art results. Negative results, resource papers, reproductions, and analyses are "in scope of the CFP and deserve a fair hearing."

**Related work assessment requires care.** The guidelines warn against H4 ("this has no precedent in literature")—more novel work is "harder to publish" and deserves fair evaluation. Authors must fairly represent existing contributions, but needn't cite preprints from the past 3 months.

## Handling Appendices and Complex Papers

**Appendices are supplementary, not required.** "The paper should be readable by itself, and any details important for understanding should be in the paper rather than appendices." Reviewers aren't obligated to review supplementary material, though consulting specific appendix sections referenced in the main text is reasonable.

**Single-dataset studies warrant context-dependent evaluation.** The guidelines emphasize that papers need "sufficient evidence for the claim that the authors are making"—not exhaustive experiments. Criticism about missing additional datasets belongs in suggestions, not rejection grounds, unless the claim fundamentally depends on broader evaluation.

## Probing and Methodology Papers

The guidelines address methodology-specific concerns through codes like M1-M5 (methodology issues) and R1-R5 (results issues). For probing studies, reviewers should verify:

- **LLM evaluation validity** (M1): If LLMs assess results, is reliability validated in this specific context?
- **Reproducibility sufficiency** (M2): Are hyperparameters, design choices, and evaluation procedures clear enough to replicate?
- **Scope-claim alignment** (R2): Do conclusions match the sample tested?

## Common Biases to Avoid

The guidelines provide "heuristics" flagging problematic reasoning:

- H7: "This method is too simple" (simplicity is preferable)
- H13: "Authors could do extra experiment X" (extra work belongs in suggestions, not rejection)
- H14: Requesting comparisons to closed-source models only makes sense if directly supporting the paper's claims

Reviewers are cautioned against convergence-to-the-mean effects when reading other reviews and advised to maintain independent judgment.
```

For probing studies, reviewers should verify:\n\n- **LLM evaluation validity** (M1): If LLMs assess results, is reliability validated in this specific context?\n- **Reproducibility sufficiency** (M2): Are hyperparameters, design choices, and evaluation procedures clear enough to replicate?\n- **Scope-claim alignment** (R2): Do conclusions match the sample tested?\n\n## Common Biases to Avoid\n\nThe guidelines provide \"heuristics\" flagging problematic reasoning:\n\n- H7: \"This method is too simple\" (simplicity is preferable)\n- H13: \"Authors could do extra experiment X\" (extra work belongs in suggestions, not rejection)\n- H14: Requesting comparisons to closed-source models only makes sense if directly supporting the paper's claims\n\nReviewers are cautioned against convergence-to-the-mean effects when reading other reviews and advised to maintain independent judgment.",

已完成全部材料阅读。现在撰写完整审稿意见。

---

## 总体评分

**Overall**: 3.0 (Findings)  
**一句话总结**: 扎实的经验性研究揭示了安全探针中指标选择对层选择的系统性偏差，但单数据集局限、ensemble 方案缺乏统计显著优势、以及 encoder 质量混淆因素共同削弱了论文对更广泛社区的影响力。

## 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）

| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |
|------|------|----------|
| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 方法论总体可靠（控制任务、bootstrap、position residualization），但单数据集+encoder质量混淆部分削弱核心claim |
| Excitement | 2.5/5 | 发现有实用价值但增量性强；对安全探针实践者有意义，但对更广泛NLP社区吸引力有限 |
| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 超参数充分报告，代码承诺公开，统计测试详尽；但数据集依赖HarmThoughts的公开性 |
| Confidence | 4/5 | 熟悉 probing methodology 和 LLM safety 文献，仔细检查了关键表格和统计测试 |

---

## 1. Summary

本文系统研究了对推理模型（reasoning models）应用 hidden-state 安全探针时，评估指标的选择如何系统性地偏移最优层的选择。在四个 7–32B 推理模型和一个安全数据集（HarmThoughts）上，作者发现：(1) threshold-based 指标（precision、inverse FPR、additive composite）选择浅层，而 crossing-rate composite 选择深层；(2) 这一偏差在 384d encoder 下显著但在 1024d encoder 下减弱；(3) 三层 ensemble 可提供指标无关的鲁棒选择。论文还报告了多个负面结果（BOCPD、Mahalanobis、cross-transfer）。

---

## 2. Strengths

**S1. 系统性的经验发现，填补实际空白。** 论文首次系统记录了安全探针研究中"指标选择→层选择→结论方向"的因果链。Table 2 的五指标×四模型热图清晰展示了 threshold-based 与 CR-composite 指标选择截然不同层的现象。这对当前 hidden-state 安全检测文献（如 TrajGuard、SafeSwitch）中常见的"在某一层取得最佳效果"的报告方式提出了有价值的方法论警示。

**S2. 严谨的统计控制和方法论自觉。** 论文采用了 Hewitt & Manning (2019) 控制任务（Section H，Appendix）验证探针并非仅捕获浅层表面特征；使用 position residualization（Section V，Appendix）排除位置信息混淆；bootstrap 10,000 次重采样加 Holm-Bonferroni 校正（Table 8）处理多重比较。这在同类安全探针论文中是高于平均水平的方法论标准。

**S3. 诚实报告负面结果。** Section 4.6 和 Appendix Q 系统报告了 BOCPD/CUSUM/Mahalanobis/RepE/MLP/CCS/cross-transfer 的失败。这些负面结果为社区节省了大量重复劳动（Table 20），且 BOCPD 在安全轨迹上的完全失败（0% detection across all configurations）本身是有信息量的。

**S4. M002 bug disclosure 体现科学诚信。** Appendix N 主动披露了 QwQ-32B text embedding pipeline 的 bug 及其修正过程，并明确说明了对结果的影响。这在机器学习论文中很少见且值得肯定。

**S5. 格式合规。** 正文 8 页，符合 EMNLP 2026 long paper 严格限制。Limitations section 详尽且位于正文后。匿名性保持良好。

---

## 3. Weaknesses

**N1 [Must-Fix] 单数据集局限严重削弱 claim 的泛化性。**
- 具体位置：Section 3.1, Table 1, Limitations Section 7
- 问题：全部实验仅使用 HarmThoughts（Kakkar et al., 2026）一个数据集。论文的核心 claim——"metric choice determines which layer appears optimal"——本质上是关于指标与层之间交互作用的结构性声明，理应在至少两个独立数据集上验证。HarmThoughts 的特定属性（step-level CP annotations, 特定 harm category 分布, 特定 reasoning chain 长度分布）可能驱动了观察到的 metric-layer 交互模式。Limitations Section 7 承认"Generalization to other jailbreak datasets, attack taxonomies, or multilingual settings is untested"，但这不仅是泛化问题，更是核心发现是否 artifact 的问题。
- 为什么影响接收：如果在另一个安全数据集（如 WildJailbreak、SafeBench、或 Do-Not-Answer）上 metric-layer 交互模式消失或显著改变，核心贡献将被大幅削弱。ARR guidelines 指出 scope-claim alignment 是关键——论文的 claim scope（"layer selection sensitivity in hidden-state safety probes"）远超其 evidence scope（"...on HarmThoughts"）。
- 建议修改方向：至少在一个额外的安全检测数据集上复现 Table 2 的核心 metric-layer 交互模式。如果不可行，应显著收窄 claim scope（如标题改为"...on HarmThoughts Benchmark"）并在 abstract/introduction 中明确这是 single-benchmark 的初步发现。

**N2 [Must-Fix] "Confirmatory" 框架的使用在统计学意义上不准确。**
- 具体位置：Section 4.2, Section 4.4 (throughout)
- 问题：论文将 threshold-based 指标的结果称为"confirmatory"（如 Section 4.2: "The 7-8B results are confirmatory"），将 CR-composite 的结果称为"exploratory"。但在统计学中，confirmatory analysis 要求预注册假设和分析计划（pre-registration）。论文描述的实际上是标准的 exploratory cross-validation，只是事后将与某些指标一致的结果标记为"confirmatory"。这创造了虚假的证据层级：threshold-based 指标的结果并不比 CR-composite 的结果具有更高的证据强度——它们只是不同指标对同一数据的不同视角。
- 为什么影响接收：这种框架可能误导读者认为论文的部分结论经过了 confirmatory validation，而实际上所有分析都是 exploratory 的。
- 建议修改方向：移除 "confirmatory/exploratory" 标签，改用更准确的描述，如 "threshold-based metrics show cross-metric convergence" vs "crossing-rate metric diverges from threshold-based metrics"。或者，如果作者确实进行了预注册，应明确说明。

**W1 [Should-Fix] Encoder 质量混淆因素未充分解决。**
- 具体位置：Section 4.3, Section 5 (Discussion, paragraph 2), Table 4
- 问题：Table 4 显示 BGE-large (1024d) 下 HS advantage 大幅减弱——R1-8B 仅 +1.7pp，QwQ-32B 仅 +1.2pp（对比 384d 下的 +17.5 和 +13.3）。Discussion Section 5 承认 "the empirical HS advantage is conditional on encoder quality, model scale, and position control"。但这引发一个根本问题：如果 encoder 足够好，是否还需要 hidden-state 探针？如果答案是否的，那么整个 metric-induced layer selection bias 的讨论就变得不那么重要了——因为最终实践者可能会选择更好的 text encoder 而非纠结于层选择。
- 建议：需要更深入讨论 encoder quality 与 HS advantage 之间的关系。是否存在一个 encoder quality threshold，超过该阈值 HS 探针不再提供增量价值？这对论文结论的实际意义有什么影响？

**W2 [Should-Fix] Three-layer ensemble 的统计支撑不足。**
- 具体位置：Section Z (Appendix, Table 36, p.28-29)
- 问题：Table 36 显示 ensemble 在所有四个模型上无一达到统计显著优于 best single metric 的结果（"No Ens-Best difference is significant (p > 0.27)"，原文 p.29）。这意味着 ensemble 的实际收益在统计上与零不可区分。作为论文 Contribution 1 的关键实践建议，这一结果令人担忧——论文实质上在推荐一个没有显著优势的方法。
- 建议：要么降低 ensemble 的推荐强度（从"provides a metric-agnostic selection"改为"offers a potential practical convenience without significant performance cost"），要么在额外数据集上验证 ensemble 的稳定性优势。

**W3 [Should-Fix] 模型规模范围有限。**
- 具体位置：Table 1, Limitations Section 7
- 问题：四个模型中三个为 7-8B，一个为 32B。没有 70B+ 模型。Limitations 中提到 "Intermediate scales, diverse base architectures, proprietary models, and multilingual settings are unicast"，但这不仅是覆盖面的问题——32B 模型（QwQ-32B）已经表现出与 7-8B 模型定性不同的行为（如 R1-32B residualized advantage 被 quadratic term 完全吸收，Section V）。更大模型的安全动态可能再次发生质变。
- 建议：至少讨论为什么 32B 的 scale 足以支持论文的方法论 claim，或者承认 layer selection sensitivity 可能是 small-model-specific 的现象。

**W4 [Should-Fix] Position 信息对 shallow-layer 优势的替代解释未充分排除。**
- 具体位置：Section V (Appendix), Table 25-26
- 问题：Position residualization 显示 3/4 模型在去除位置信息后仍保持 HS advantage，但 R1-32B 是例外——其 residualized advantage 仅 +1.9pp (p=0.191)，且被 quadratic term 完全吸收（Table 26）。这暗示至少在部分模型上，shallow-layer probes 的优势可能主要由位置信息驱动，而非真正的安全语义信号。
- 建议：更明确地讨论 R1-32B 的位置依赖性，及其对 "metric-induced layer selection bias" narrative 的影响。

**M1 [Minor] 部分 claim 语气过强。**
- Section 1 Abstract: "We show this choice alone can reverse a study's central conclusions" — 但论文实际展示的是 metric 选择改变了哪一层被选为最优，而非任何已发表论文的结论被实际反转。"Can reverse" 是假设性的，缺乏具体实例。
- Section 6 Conclusion: "Contribution 2 (HS advantage magnitude) is initial characterization on one benchmark requiring further validation" — 这句话的诚实度值得肯定，但也暴露了论文最大弱点。

**M2 [Minor] Table 2 热图的可读性。**
- Figure 1 (p.6) 的热图颜色区分度有限，特别是对色觉障碍读者。建议使用 diverging colormap 并增大标注字体。

---

## 4. Questions for Authors

**Q1.** 如果在 WildJailbreak 或 Do-Not-Answer 等替代数据集上重复 Table 2 的实验，你们预测 metric-layer 交互模式会保持还是改变？为什么？

**Q2.** Table 36 显示 ensemble 的优势在所有模型上都不显著。在这种情况下，为什么仍将 "three-layer ensemble" 作为论文的核心 practical recommendation？

**Q3.** 论文使用 "confirmatory" 一词时，是否进行了任何形式的假设预注册（pre-registration）？如果没有，是否愿意更改这一用语？

**Q4.** 给定 BGE-large (1024d) 下 HS advantage 基本消失（Table 4），论文的核心发现（metric-induced bias）在更强 encoder 下是否仍有实际意义？即：如果 practitioner 使用 1024d encoder，层选择还重要吗？

**Q5.** R1-32B 的 position residualization 结果（Table 26, quadratic term 完全吸收 HS advantage）如何与论文的主要 narrative（shallow layers capture genuine safety signal）共存？

**Q6.** Control task selectivity（Section H）显示 R1-8B 在 threshold-based layers 的 selectivity 仅 3.0pp，低于 Hewitt & Manning (2019) 推荐的 5pp threshold。这是否应被视为 R1-8B probes 未通过 control task validation 的证据？

---

## 5. Missing References

**R1.** **Zou et al. (2024), "Improving Alignment and Robustness with Circuit Breakers"** — 与本文的 safety probe 方向高度相关，使用 representation-level intervention 进行安全控制，但未被引用。

**R2.** **Templeton et al. (2024), "Scaling Monosemanticity: Extracting Interpretable Features from Claude 3 Sonnet"** — Anthropic 的大规模特征提取工作，与理解模型内部安全表征直接相关。虽然方法论不同（SAE vs linear probes），但讨论中应至少提及这一方向。

**R3.** **Nanda et al. (2023), "Progress measures for grokking via mechanistic interpretability"** — 虽然不直接关于安全，但其关于不同层表征质量随训练演变的发现与本文的层选择敏感性主题有方法论联系。

---

## 6. Overall Assessment

**Overall: 3.0 (Findings)**  
**Confidence: 4/5**

本文提供了一个有价值的方法论警示——安全探针研究中的指标选择会系统性偏移层选择和结论方向。实验设计严谨，控制变量充分，负面结果报告诚实。但三个核心弱点阻碍了 Conference 级别的接收：(1) 单数据集无法支撑论文 claim 的泛化性；(2) 核心实践建议（ensemble）缺乏统计显著优势；(3) encoder 质量混淆因素暗示整个 HS probe 范式的增量价值可能有限。

如果作者能在 rebuttal 中提供至少一个额外数据集上的 metric-layer 交互模式复现结果，且 ensemble 在新数据集上展示显著优势，评分可上调至 3.5 (Borderline Conference)。

---

## 7. Detailed Comments (按 Section)

**Abstract & Introduction (p.1)**
- Abstract 信息密度很高，几乎涵盖了所有核心发现，但读起来更像 results summary 而非 abstract。建议用 1-2 句话清晰陈述 research question，而非直接列举结果。
- Introduction 的 "confirmatory" vs "exploratory" 框架在首次出现时缺乏定义（直到 Section 4.2 才解释），建议在 Introduction 中简要定义。

**Related Work (Section 2, p.2-3)**
- 覆盖全面，引用了 TrajGuard、SafeSwitch、SafeRemind、SIREN、SafeDream 等最新工作。Probing methodology 文献（Hewitt & Manning, Voita & Titov, Pimentel et al., Belinkov 2022）也有充分引用。
- "Alternative Detection Approaches" 段落（p.3）将 BOCPD、RepE、CCS、cross-transfer 并列讨论，但未区分这些方法的根本差异（temporal vs spatial vs cross-model），导致读者不清楚为什么这些都被归为"alternative"。

**Method (Section 3, p.3-4)**
- Safety Commitment Point (CP) 的定义（Section 3.1）清晰，但 "first reasoning step after which safety reasoning permanently ceases" 的 "permanently" 假设过强。论文自己在 Limitations 中承认存在 oscillating patterns。
- Five metrics 的选择（Section 3.3）有充分理由：balanced accuracy 和 crossing rate 来自不同评估维度，threshold-based metrics 提供操作化的部署指标。但未讨论为什么不考虑 AUROC 作为 threshold-free 的替代指标——仅在 p.6 简要提到 AUROC 在 BA-selected vs threshold-based layers 的差异。

**Experiments (Section 4, p.4-7)**
- Table 2 是论文最有价值的结果，清晰展示了 metric-layer dissociation。
- Table 3 (p.6) 的 HS vs text 比较设计合理，但 10,000 bootstrap resamples 的 BCa confidence intervals 仅在部分模型上 significant（R1-32B exploratory: p value not reported）。
- Section 4.4 的 model-specific analysis 信息量大但略显冗长（p.7），部分内容可移至 appendix。
- Section 4.5 的 temporal context analysis 简短有效，window ablation 结果清晰。

**Discussion (Section 5, p.8)**
- "Layer selection as researcher degree of freedom" 的框架很好，但未引用 Gelman & Loken (2013) "Garden of Forking Paths"——而此文恰好在 bib 中（orphan entry: `gelman2013garden`，未被任何 \cite 引用）。这是一个明显的遗漏：该引用与 Section 5 的论点直接相关。
- "Encoder-quality boundary" 段落（p.8）是论文最坦诚的自我批评，但应在 abstract 中有所反映。

**Limitations (Section 7, p.9)**
- 全面且诚实。CP assumption、hyperparameter sensitivity、single dataset、model-specific caveats 都有涉及。
- "Model-specific caveats and statistical power" 段落（p.9）中 "R1-32B achieves significance only at W=1 (n=251), at H≥2, dimensionality prevents significance" 这一点对 R1-32B 所有结论都是重要的 caveat，但在主文中未充分强调。

**Appendix**
- 内容丰富（17 页），涵盖了大量 robustness checks。但部分 section 标签不够直观（Section V = Position-Residualized Probing, Section Z = Multi-Metric Layer Ensemble），建议使用更描述性的标签。
- Appendix N (M002 Bug Disclosure) 的透明度值得赞赏。

---

现在将审稿意见提交给 Review Director。

```json
{
  "text": "## Reviewer 2 审稿意见：Layer Selection Sensitivity in Hidden-State Safety Probes for Reasoning Models\n\n### 总体评分\n**Overall**: 3.0 (Findings)\n**Confidence**: 4/5\n**一句话总结**: 扎实的经验性研究揭示了安全探针中指标选择对层选择的系统性偏差，但单数据集局限、ensemble 方案缺乏统计显著优势、以及 encoder 质量混淆因素共同削弱了论文对更广泛社区的影响力。\n\n### 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 方法论总体可靠（控制任务、bootstrap、position residualization），但单数据集+encoder质量混淆部分削弱核心claim |\n| Excitement | 2.5/5 | 发现有实用价值但增量性强；对安全探针实践者有意义，但对更广泛NLP社区吸引力有限 |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 超参数充分报告，代码承诺公开，统计测试详尽 |\n\n---\n\n### Summary\n本文系统研究了对推理模型应用 hidden-state 安全探针时，评估指标的选择如何系统性地偏移最优层的选择。在四个 7–32B 推理模型和 HarmThoughts 数据集上，作者发现：(1) threshold-based 指标选择浅层而 crossing-rate composite 选择深层；(2) 偏差在 384d encoder 下显著但在 1024d encoder 下减弱；(3) 三层 ensemble 可提供指标无关的鲁棒选择。论文还报告了多个负面结果。\n\n---\n\n### Strengths\n\n**S1. 系统性的经验发现。** Table 2 的五指标×四模型热图清晰展示了 threshold-based 与 CR-composite 指标选择截然不同层的现象，对当前 hidden-state 安全检测文献中\"在某一层取得最佳效果\"的报告方式提出有价值的方法论警示。\n\n**S2. 严谨的统计控制。** Hewitt & Manning 控制任务验证（Section H）、position residualization（Section V）、bootstrap 10,000 次+Holm-Bonferroni 校正（Table 8）——在同类安全探针论文中高于平均水平。\n\n**S3. 诚实报告负面结果。** Section 4.6 和 Appendix Q 系统报告了 BOCPD/CUSUM/Mahalanobis/RepE/MLP/CCS/cross-transfer 的失败，为社区节省重复劳动。\n\n**S4. M002 bug disclosure 体现科学诚信。** Appendix N 主动披露 bug 及修正过程，在 ML 论文中罕见且值得肯定。\n\n**S5. 格式合规。** 正文 8 页，符合 EMNLP 2026 long paper 限制。匿名性良好。\n\n---\n\n### Must-Fix 问题\n\n**N1 [critical] 单数据集局限严重削弱 claim 的泛化性。**\n- 具体位置：Section 3.1, Table 1, Limitations Section 7\n- 全部实验仅使用 HarmThoughts 一个数据集。论文的核心 claim——metric choice determines optimal layer——本质上是关于指标与层之间交互作用的结构性声明，理应在至少两个独立数据集上验证。HarmThoughts 的特定属性可能驱动了观察到的模式。claim scope 远超 evidence scope。\n- 建议：至少在一个额外安全数据集上复现 Table 2 的核心模式，或显著收窄 claim scope。\n\n**N2 [critical] \"Confirmatory\" 框架在统计学意义上不准确。**\n- 具体位置：Section 4.2, Section 4.4\n- 论文将 threshold-based 指标结果称为\"confirmatory\"，但未进行预注册。这是标准 exploratory analysis 的事后标记，创造虚假证据层级。\n- 建议：移除 confirmatory/exploratory 标签，改用\"cross-metric convergence\" vs \"metric divergence\"等更准确的描述。\n\n---\n\n### Should-Fix 问题\n\n**W1 [warning] Encoder 质量混淆因素未充分解决。**\n- 具体位置：Section 4.3, Table 4\n- BGE-large (1024d) 下 HS advantage 大幅减弱（R1-8B 仅 +1.7pp）。如果 encoder 足够好就不需要 HS 探针，则 metric-induced layer selection bias 的讨论变得不那么重要。\n\n**W2 [warning] Three-layer ensemble 的统计支撑不足。**\n- 具体位置：Table 36 (Appendix Z, p.28-29)\n- 所有模型上 ensemble 都不显著优于 best single metric（p > 0.27）。核心 practical recommendation 缺乏统计显著支持。\n\n**W3 [warning] 模型规模范围有限。**\n- 三个 7-8B + 一个 32B，无 70B+ 模型。32B 已表现出定性不同行为，更大模型可能再次质变。\n\n**W4 [warning] R1-32B 的 position residualization 暗示 shallow-layer 优势可能由位置驱动。**\n- Table 26 显示 R1-32B 的 HS advantage 被 quadratic position term 完全吸收，与其他模型不一致。\n\n---\n\n### Minor 问题\n- gelman2013garden 在 bib 中但未被引用——而 Section 5 \"researcher degree of freedom\" 的讨论正好需要此引用\n- Abstract 信息密度过高，更像 results summary 而非 abstract\n- Figure 1 热图对色觉障碍读者不友好\n- Appendix section 标签不够描述性（V, Z 等字母编号不直观）\n- Section 1 \"We show this choice alone can reverse a study's central conclusions\" 语气过强，论文未展示任何已发表论文的结论被实际反转\n\n---\n\n### Questions for Authors\n1. 如果在 WildJailbreak 等替代数据集上重复 Table 2，metric-layer 交互模式会保持吗？\n2. Ensemble 无统计显著优势，为何仍作为核心 practical recommendation？\n3. \"Confirmatory\" 一词是否基于任何预注册？\n4. BGE-large 下 HS advantage 基本消失，层选择在强 encoder 下还重要吗？\n5. R1-32B position residualization 如何与 \"shallow layers capture genuine safety signal\" 共存？\n6. R1-8B 控制任务 selectivity 仅 3.0pp（低于 5pp threshold），是否应视为未通过验证？\n\n---\n\n### Missing References\n1. Zou et al. (2024), \"Improving Alignment and Robustness with Circuit Breakers\" — representation-level safety intervention\n2. Templeton et al. (2024), \"Scaling Monosemanticity\" — 大规模特征提取，理解安全表征\n3. gelman2013garden 已在 bib 中但未引用，Section 5 的 \"researcher degree of freedom\" 讨论应引用此文\n\n---\n\n### Detailed Comments by Section\n\n**Introduction**: \"confirmatory/exploratory\" 框架首次出现时缺定义。Abstract 应反映 encoder quality boundary 这一重要 caveat。\n\n**Related Work**: 覆盖全面，但 \"Alternative Detection Approaches\" 段未区分 temporal/spatial/cross-model 方法的根本差异。\n\n**Method**: CP 定义中 \"permanently ceases\" 假设过强（论文自己承认 oscillating patterns）。未讨论为何不考虑 AUROC 作为 threshold-free 替代。\n\n**Experiments**: Table 2 是最有价值的结果。Section 4.4 model-specific analysis 略冗长，部分可移至 appendix。\n\n**Discussion**: \"Researcher degree of freedom\" 框架好但应引用 Gelman & Loken (2013)。\"Encoder-quality boundary\" 是最坦诚的自我批评，应在 abstract 有所反映。\n\n**Limitations**: 全面诚实。R1-32B 的统计功效限制应在主文中更强调。\n\n**Appendix**: 内容丰富（17页），robustness checks 充分。Section labels 建议更描述性。",
```

## Reviewer 1 审稿意见

### Overall: 3.5 (Borderline Conference), Confidence 4/5

### Strengths
S1. 重要且扎实的方法论贡献——层选择作为 researcher degree of freedom 可逆转 HS vs. text 结论。五度量消融+random-label控制。
S2. 统计方法论远超同类工作——HB校正、nested CV、BCa bootstrap、MDE计算、trace-level聚类。
S3. 负面结果的系统性报告——7种替代方法失败 + 四个信号特征要求。
S4. 诚实局限性披露和 bug 透明度——R1-32B/QwQ-32B 标记 exploratory，Appendix N bug disclosure。
S5. 三层 ensemble 概念验证——4/4模型显著（p≤0.002）。

### Must-Fix
N1 [critical] 单一数据集限制外部效度——仅 HarmThoughts，无法区分是表示几何基本属性还是数据集特有产物。
N2 [critical] 缺乏 IAA——CP标注质量无法评估，系统性标注偏差不会被 Appendix J 扰动分析捕获。
N3 [critical] 32B 样本量极小（n=19-20）——承载过重方法论负荷，R1-32B 残差化优势+1.9pp 远低于 MDE，25,600维输入仅60训练样本。

### Should-Fix
W1 [warning] 位置基线威胁核心 HS 优势——2/4模型 HS 优势被位置变量吸收或严重削弱。
W2 [warning] MiniLM-384d 是弱基线——维度和容量严重不匹配，1024d下仅1/4模型通过HB。
W3 [warning] HB 校正范围可能不充分——总研究者自由度远大于12个测试。
W4 [warning] 3/4 模型共享 Qwen 架构——架构多样性有限。
W5 [warning] CP 定义循环性——需观察完整 trace 才能识别 CP，与在线检测目标存在概念张力。
W6 [warning] FPR 过高（11-50%）——与实际部署叙事不匹配。

### Minor
m1. 摘要密度过高
m2. Figure 1 太小
m3. "confirmatory" 术语可能与标准统计学混淆
m4. 18/20 通过率的2个失败案例未清楚标注
m5. 孤儿 bib 条目 gelman2013garden
m6. geiger2025causal 年份可能有误

### Questions
Q1. 证据层级分类是事前还是事后确定？
Q2. 为何线性规格优先于二次规格？HS探针是否可能仅是更好的位置编码器？
Q3. Nested CV 层选择不稳定，CR-composite 偏差是否只是一般不稳定性的实例？
Q4. 32B 模型 W=5 输入 25,600 维但仅60训练样本，如何确保不过拟合？
Q5. 结果在不同 harmful categories 间是否稳定？
Q6. 为何 K=5 固定？

### Missing References
- Turner et al., 2023 activation addition
- Chan & Yong (2025) 更详细讨论

It's a valid point but the paper does provide some mitigation through this analysis.

The minimum detectable effect at 32B scale is 8.6-9.5 percentage points according to Table 35, which means smaller effects can't be reliably distinguished. This is a serious limitation that the paper acknowledges.

```text
On W1 about the position baseline, only 2 out of 4 models show the effect, which is the core issue here.

For W2 on the MiniLM encoder, it's true that 384 dimensions is quite weak compared to the 5120 hidden states, making the comparison potentially unfair. But the paper also tests BGE-large at 1024 dimensions and shows the hidden state advantage diminishes there, which actually supports the point that the advantage might stem from encoder weakness rather than being a fundamental property.

On W3 about Bonferroni correction, the concern is valid—the paper only corrects for 12 tests across 4 models and 3 metrics, but the actual analysis space is much larger when you account for layer sweeps, different metrics, models, window sizes, and thresholds. The nested cross-validation structure provides some mitigation, though it doesn't fully address the multiple comparisons problem.

On W5 about the circular definition of checkpoints—the paper requires observing the complete trace to identify them, which conflicts with the online detection use case they describe. But since the paper's contribution is methodological rather than a deployment system, using the checkpoint as ground truth for training and evaluation is reasonable.

For W6 on false positive rates, Table 3 and the threshold ablation in Table 18 show that FPR varies significantly by model at K=5, which the paper acknowledges in their deployment operating points discussion. This remains a legitimate concern for practical applications.

Looking at the reviewer questions now: Q1 aligns with my earlier concern about whether confirmatory versus exploratory analysis was predetermined. Q2 raises whether the HS probes are just better position encoders—the paper addresses this through position residualization, though R1-32B's results are still troubling. For Q3, the reviewer questions whether CR-composite's different layer selection reflects genuine differences or just noise from LOO-CV instability. The 65-68% layer agreement at 7-8B is relatively low, and while Table 5 shows joint layer/W stability peaks at L63 for QwQ-32B, I need to examine whether cross-metric consistency supports the layer choices.

The systematic disagreement between threshold-based selection (favoring shallow layers) and CR-composite (favoring deep layers) suggests this isn't random noise—it's a real methodological difference. Q4 flags severe overfitting risk with 25,600 dimensions and only 60 training samples, even with L2 regularization. Q5 notes the paper lacks category-level stability breakdowns. _source line 173 · 2026-05-19T17:05:30.932Z_
```

逐条回应 Reviewer 1 的审稿意见。

---

### Strengths 回应

**[AGREE] S1**: 核心方法论贡献确实重要。Table 2 清晰展示了 metric-layer dissociation，我在自己的审稿中也将其列为最有价值的结果（我的 S1）。

**[AGREE] S2**: 统计方法论水平确实远超同类工作。我同样在自己的审稿中突出了这一点（我的 S2），特别是 Hewitt & Manning 控制任务 + position residualization + Holm-Bonferroni 校正的组合。

**[AGREE] S3**: 负面结果报告的价值毫无疑问。Table 20 (Appendix Q) 的七方法系统失败对社区有实际节省重复劳动的价值（我的 S3）。

**[AGREE] S4**: Bug disclosure 的科学诚信值得肯定（我的 S4）。

**[REBUT] S5**: R1 称 "三层 ensemble 4/4模型显著（p≤0.002）"，但这有误导性。Table 36 (p.29) 明确报告："All Ens-Test differences are significant (p ≤ 0.002, permutation test). **No Ens-Best difference is significant (p > 0.27).**" 即 ensemble 显著优于 text baseline（Ens-Test），但**不显著优于 best single metric**（Ens-Best）。这是关键区别——ensemble 的实际增量价值（相对于已有最佳单指标）在统计上与零不可区分。R1 的表述遗漏了后半句，可能误导 AC。

---

### Must-Fix 回应

**[AGREE] R1-N1**: 单数据集限制。我提出了完全相同的问题（我的 N1），且给出了相同的建议——至少在一个额外数据集上复现 Table 2 的 metric-layer 交互模式，或收窄 claim scope。这是两位 reviewer 的一致判断，应被视为高优先级。

**[AGREE] R1-N2**: IAA 缺失是合理关切。不过我要补充两个 nuance：
1. HarmThoughts 的 CP annotations 来自 Kakkar et al. (2026) 的原始数据集，IAA 报告的责任部分在原始论文而非本文。
2. Appendix J 的 CP perturbation analysis（±1-4 steps）虽然不能替代 IAA，但确实提供了标注鲁棒性的间接证据——论文报告 "precision degrades smoothly with no cliff effect: within ±2 steps, BA changes by <2.5pp"（p.19-20）。
总体而言，我认为 IAA 是 should-fix 而非 must-fix 级别：perturbation analysis 提供了部分覆盖，且这是使用已有 benchmark 的研究而非创建新标注。

**[AGREE] R1-N3**: 32B 样本量问题严重。Table 1 显示 R1-32B 仅 n=19-20（经 canonical 60/20/20 split 后训练集更小），Table 35 (Appendix) 显示 MDE 为 8.6-9.5pp——论文报告的多个效应远小于此 MDE。25,600 维输入（W=5 × 5120d）对应仅约 60 训练样本的维度/样本比极端失衡，即使 L2 正则化也难以完全缓解。我在自己的审稿中标注为 W3 (should-fix)，但接受 R1 将其升级为 must-fix 的判断——32B 结果承担了过重的方法论负荷（论文用 R1-32B vs QwQ-32B 的差异来论证 scale-dependent effects）。

---

### Should-Fix 回应

**[AGREE] R1-W1**: Position baseline 威胁 HS 优势。我提出了同一问题（我的 W4）。Table 26 显示 R1-32B 的 HS advantage 被 quadratic position term 完全吸收（p.25），这是对核心 narrative 的直接威胁。2/4 模型的位置依赖性不能简单归为"模型特异性"——它可能是规律。

**[AGREE] R1-W2**: MiniLM-384d 作为基线的问题。与我的 W1 (encoder quality confound) 一致。384d vs 5120d hidden states 的维度不匹配使得 HS advantage 的一部分可能仅反映信息容量差异。论文自身的 1024d BGE-large 结果（Table 4）佐证了这一点：HS advantage 大幅减弱。

**[REBUT] R1-W3**: HB 校正范围不充分的论断需要 nuance。R1 认为 "总研究者自由度远大于12个测试"。这在原则上正确，但论文的 HB 校正是针对 Table 8 中的特定假设族（4 models × 3 threshold-based metrics = 12 HS vs text comparisons）。Nested CV 的层选择（Section B）和 metric 选择是在 inner loop 中完成的，不属于最终假设检验的自由度——这是 nested CV 设计的目的。当然，论文确实在 Table 8 之外报告了大量 p-values（position residualization、control task、ensemble 等），这些构成了额外的检验族。但 R1 提出的应该对**所有**分析统一 HB 校正的建议在实践中不可行，也不是标准做法。我认为这是 minor 级别，不应升至 should-fix。

**[AGREE] R1-W4**: 3/4 Qwen 架构的多样性限制。Table 1 确认 OT-7B (Qwen2.5-7B based)、QwQ-32B (Qwen)、R1-32B (Qwen-32B) 都基于 Qwen 架构，仅 R1-8B 是 Llama。如果 metric-layer dissociation 是 Qwen 架构的特有属性而非通用现象，核心贡献的泛化性将大打折扣。不过论文的 R1-8B (Llama) 也展示了相同的 metric-layer 模式（Table 2），提供了部分跨架构证据。我同意这是 should-fix 但非 must-fix——作者应在 Limitations 中明确讨论架构多样性限制。

**[REBUT] R1-W5**: CP 定义循环性的论断需要区分**研究目标**和**部署场景**。论文的核心贡献是方法论层面的——揭示 probing 研究中 metric 选择对层选择的影响——而非提出一个在线检测系统。CP 作为事后标注的 ground truth 用于训练和评估探针的行为，这是 probing 研究的标准范式（类似于 POS tagging probe 需要事后标注的 ground truth，但 probe 本身可用于在线预测）。论文在 Section 3.5 (Evaluation Protocol) 中明确了评估是 per-step 的，探针在每一步独立预测 pre-CP vs post-CP，不需要看到完整 trace。Appendix I (Oracle Upper Bound) 进一步分析了理论上的在线检测上限。我认为这一批评混淆了 annotation protocol 和 inference protocol。

**[AGREE] R1-W6**: FPR 过高确实与部署叙事不匹配。Table 18 (Appendix L) 显示 K=5 时 safe FPR 在 4.8-16.5% 之间（模型依赖），Appendix L.1 的部署操作点分析承认 "65-86% coverage with 30-58% safe FPR at K=10"。论文在 Discussion 中提到 "a secondary review system could inspect only flagged traces"，但对于宣称 safety monitoring 实用性的论文来说，这个 FPR 水平确实需要更诚实的定位。

---

### Questions 回应

**[AGREE] R1-Q1**: "Confirmatory/exploratory" 的证据层级是否事前确定。我提出了同一问题（我的 N2）——论文未提及任何预注册。如果这一分类是事后的，使用 "confirmatory" 标签就是误导性的。

**[AGREE] R1-Q2**: 线性 vs 二次规格的问题很好。Table 26 (Appendix V) 显示 QwQ-32B 的 advantage 被二次项完全吸收。R1 提出的 "HS probes 可能仅是更好的位置编码器" 是一个我没有明确提出但完全认同的替代假设。论文应更认真地讨论这一可能性。

**[REBUT] R1-Q3**: CR-composite 偏差是否仅是一般不稳定性的实例。我认为不是。Table 2 的模式是**系统性的**：threshold-based metrics（precision、inverse FPR、additive composite）在所有四个模型上一致选择浅层（L14 附近 for 7-8B），而 CR-composite 在所有模型上一致选择深层（L16 for 7-8B, L63 for 32B）。如果这仅是随机不稳定性，我们不会看到如此一致的 metric-direction 关联。此外，Table 5 (p.14) 的 LOO-CV joint (layer, W) stability 显示 QwQ-32B 在 L63 处 near-optimal across 192 configurations。CR-composite 的深层偏好有可解释的机制原因——deep layers 的表征更频繁跨越 threshold crossings（论文 Section 4.2），inflate crossing rate。

**[AGREE] R1-Q4**: 32B W=5 的过拟合风险。25,600 维输入 / ~60 训练样本的比例（约 427:1）极端失衡。L2 正则化有所帮助但不能完全解决。论文应报告 32B 模型在不同 W 值下的训练/验证 accuracy gap 来量化过拟合程度。

**[AGREE] R1-Q5**: Category-level stability 是合理的 gap。HarmThoughts 涵盖 violence、fraud、illegal activities 等多个 harm categories。论文未报告 metric-layer 交互模式是否在不同 categories 间稳定。如果某些 categories 的模式与总体不同，核心发现的解释需要修正。

**[REBUT] R1-Q6**: K=5 的选择有明确说明。Appendix L (p.20-21) 报告了 K∈{1,3,5,7,10} 的完整 ablation（Table 18）。K=5 是在 R1-8B validation set 上选择并 held fixed across all models。选择逻辑是 coverage-timing tradeoff："K=5 yields perfect coverage and near-perfect safe FPR"（p.20）。论文确实可以更多讨论 K 选择的敏感性，但 "为何 K=5 固定" 这一问题在 Appendix L 中有答案。

---

### Missing References 回应

**[AGREE]** Turner et al. (2023) activation addition 与论文的 representation intervention 讨论（Section 5, citing Li et al. 2024 ITI）相关，值得在 Related Work 中提及。

**[REBUT]** Chan & Yong (2025) 已在论文中被引用（Section 5 Discussion: "Chan and Yong (2025) probe safety commitment in LLMs"），R1 要求"更详细讨论"。我查看了论文原文——Section 5 最后一段确实引用了 Chan & Yong (2025)，但仅一句话。鉴于该工作直接研究 safety commitment probing，更详细的对比讨论（方法差异、发现是否一致）是合理的建议，但不是严重遗漏。

---

### 与 R1 的评分差异说明

R1 给出 3.5 (Borderline Conference)，我给出 3.0 (Findings)。0.5 分差异主要来自两个分歧：

1. **Ensemble 评价**：R1 对 ensemble 的评价偏正面（S5），但如上所述 Ens-Best 差异不显著。我认为这削弱了论文的 practical contribution。
2. **Excitement**：R1 未明确给出 excitement 分数。我的 excitement 评分为 2.5/5——核心发现（metric 选择影响层选择）有实用价值但增量性强，对不从事安全探针研究的 NLP 研究者吸引力有限。

两位 reviewer 在 N1（单数据集）上完全一致，这应给 AC 强信号。

```json
  "text": "## Reviewer 2 对 Reviewer 1 意见的交叉回应\n\n### Strengths\n- [AGREE] S1-S4: 与我的评估一致\n- [REBUT] S5: R1 称 \"ensemble 4/4显著(p≤0.002)\"有误导。Table 36 明确区分了 Ens-Test（vs text baseline，显著）和 Ens-Best（vs best single metric，**不显著 p>0.27**）。Ensemble 的增量价值与零不可区分。\n\n### Must-Fix\n- [AGREE] R1-N1: 单数据集限制——与我的 N1 完全一致。两位 reviewer 一致意见。\n- [AGREE] R1-N2 (部分): IAA 缺失是合理关切，但我认为是 should-fix 而非 must-fix。CP annotations 来自原始 HarmThoughts benchmark，Appendix J perturbation (±1-4 steps) 提供了间接鲁棒性证据。\n- [AGREE] R1-N3: 32B 样本量问题严重。MDE 8.6-9.5pp（Table 35）意味着论文报告的多个效应（如 R1-32B 残差化 +1.9pp）远低于可检测阈值。\n\n### Should-Fix\n- [AGREE] R1-W1: Position baseline 威胁——同我的 W4\n- [AGREE] R1-W2: MiniLM-384d 弱基线——同我的 W1\n- [REBUT] R1-W3: HB 校正范围。Nested CV 内循环的选择不属于最终假设检验自由度，这是 nested CV 设计的目的。应为 minor 而非 should-fix。\n- [AGREE] R1-W4: 3/4 Qwen 架构确实限制多样性\n- [REBUT] R1-W5: CP 循环性混淆了 annotation protocol 和 inference protocol。论文是 probing 研究而非部署系统，CP 作为 ground truth 的使用是标准范式。\n- [AGREE] R1-W6: FPR 11-50% 与部署叙事不匹配\n\n### Questions\n- [AGREE] Q1,Q2,Q4,Q5: 有效问题\n- [REBUT] Q3: CR-composite 偏差是系统性的（Table 2 中所有模型一致），非随机不稳定性\n- [REBUT] Q6: K=5 选择在 Appendix L 有完整 ablation 和理由\n\n### 评分差异\nR1: 3.5, R2: 3.0。差异来自：(1) 我对 ensemble 评价更审慎（Ens-Best 不显著）；(2) excitement 2.5/5（增量性发现）。\n\n两位 reviewer 在 N1（单数据集）完全一致，建议 AC 将此列为最高优先修改项。",
```
